Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a tumors algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 711. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 332/3.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on tumors structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 711. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 332. QED.
